# Compute

> Machines, sandboxes, notebooks, GPU time, platform hosts and how long each runs before it is reaped.

1,524 records: 523 usable, 159 trial, 549 paid only, 176 rejected, 81 dead; 36 self-hosted of any group, listed last. This page lists the usable, trial and paid-only records; the rejected and dead ones are on https://fshy.ajam.dev/t/compute/rejected.md. Each line: provider (its page in Markdown), record id (its JSON is https://fshy.ajam.dev/api/v1/record/<id>), verdict, free tier, identity, card, phone, evidence, stale date, parsed price, summary. In full: https://fshy.ajam.dev/t/compute/llms-full.txt.

Summaries are cut to 3 characters to keep this page within 300 KB, about 81K tokens; each provider page has them whole.

Best per need: [Free or trial GPU time](https://fshy.ajam.dev/need/free-gpu.md), [Free compute with no card](https://fshy.ajam.dev/need/compute-no-card.md), [Cheapest rented machines by USD a month](https://fshy.ajam.dev/need/cheapest-machines.md)
